The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


### [248] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuoteBench 评估 LLM 编码代理在 Bash 命令生成与执行传输边界上的失效来源，发现匹配执行分数会掩盖传输层引入的损害与模型适应带来的补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常只看命令最终是否执行成功，难以区分错误来自模型生成阶段，还是来自接口序列化、包装、重新解析等执行传输过程。论文希望测量这一边界对 LLM 命令执行可靠性的影响。

Method: 构建 QuoteBench：包含 56 个一次性任务，来自 14 类事故衍生任务族；交叉考察生成契约与执行传输路径，并在传输中加入一个刻意未转义的额外解析器。使用精确最终状态验证来判定成功，同时比较原始路径、加入解析器后的重放路径，以及披露边界后模型是否能通过改变生成策略恢复。

Result: 在八种同窗口配置中，将同一回复经额外解析器重放会使成功率下降 55.4 到 73.2 个百分点；披露边界后，六种配置恢复 30.4 到 60.7 个百分点，另外两种无恢复或略微下降。GPT-5.6-sol 的匹配差距仅为 -3.6 点，但实际掩盖了 -64.3 点损害和 +60.7 点补偿。部署配置还会改变模型排序。

Conclusion: 命令执行代理的评估不应把单一匹配分数视为模型固有能力，而应报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、操作点和最终状态验证器；模型间差异在前沿更体现为对边界条件的适应能力。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [New optimal linear codes over $\ZZ_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12414)
*Hopein Christofen Tang,Djoko Suprijanto*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出从已知的 \(\mathbb{Z}_4\) 线性码构造新线性码的方法，并获得了多种新的、其中许多为最优的码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线性码理论中，构造具有更优参数的新码是核心问题。作者关注 \(\mathbb{Z}_4\) 上线性码的构造，希望利用已有码生成新的最优或接近最优码。

Method: 提出若干基于已知 \(\mathbb{Z}_4\) 线性码的新构造方法，用这些方法系统地产生新的线性码，并分析其参数与最优性。

Result: 获得了许多新的 \(\mathbb{Z}_4\) 线性码，其中大量为最优码；特别是找到了 \(k_1=2, k_2=0\) 情况下的所有最优码，以及 \(k_1=3, k_2=0\) 情况下的许多最优码。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了 \(\mathbb{Z}_4\) 线性码的构造技术与已知最优码表，证明新构造方法在发现最优码方面具有有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we present novel approaches for constructing linear codes over $\ZZ_4$ from the known ones. We succeeded in obtaining new linear codes, many of which are optimal. In particular, we found all optimal codes for $k_1=2,~k_2=0$ and many optimal codes for $k_1=3,~k_2=0.$

</details>


### [250] [Channel Estimation for OTFS Systems With Overspread Doppler Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12524)
*Preety Priya,Yi Hong,Emanuele Viterbo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对非地面多卫星链路中 OTFS 系统的过扩展多普勒信道估计问题，提出含余弦导频与 DD 域导频符号的训练帧及两阶段估计方法，以恢复真实多普勒、时延和信道增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时变信道中，若多普勒频移超过子载波间隔的一半，OTFS 的模运算会导致 DD 域多普勒混叠，使传统信道估计方法失效；该问题在非地面多卫星链路中尤为典型。

Method: 设计由余弦导频信号和导频符号组成的 DD 训练帧。第一阶段利用 DD 域导频符号估计传播路径的时延、混叠多普勒和信道增益；第二阶段将接收时域信号转换到频域，利用余弦导频检测真实多普勒峰值，并通过基于阈值的配对方法将真实多普勒与对应时延和信道增益匹配，同时分析算法复杂度。

Result: 在多种场景下，通过归一化均方误差（NMSE）和误码率（BER）验证了所提信道估计方法的性能，表明其能够有效处理过扩展多普勒导致的混叠问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为过扩展多普勒条件下的 OTFS 信道估计提供了一种可行方案，特别适用于非地面多卫星等高速、强时变通信场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider an orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) system in time-varying channels with overspread Doppler shifts, typically found in non-terrestrial multi-satellite links. The overspread Doppler shifts with magnitude greater than half of the subcarrier spacing, result in aliased Doppler shifts in the delay-Doppler (DD) domain due to the OTFS modulo operation. This makes channel estimation very challenging and the traditional channel estimation methods become ineffective. To address this challenge, we propose a DD training frame and a two-stage channel estimation method. The training frame comprises a cosine pilot signal and a pilot symbol. In the first stage of the channel estimation, the pilot symbol in the DD domain is utilized to estimate the delays, aliased Doppler shifts, and channel gains of the propagation paths. In the second stage, the received time domain signal is converted into the frequency domain to detect the peaks of all the Doppler shifts using the cosine pilot signal. Then, we present a threshold-based method to pair the estimated actual Doppler shifts with their corresponding delays and channel gains. The complexity of the proposed channel estimation is also discussed. Finally, the performance of the proposed channel estimation is validated in terms of the normalized mean square error (NMSE) and bit error rate (BER) in various scenarios.

</details>


### [251] [On Nearly-Perfect Covering Codes Beyond Radius One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12595)
*Gabriel Sac Himelfarb,Moshe Schwartz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究二元 nearly-perfect covering codes，指出原始 Van Wee 界在覆盖半径 R≥2 时无法被严格达到，并提出考虑最小距离的改进界，同时完成 R=2,3 情形的分类并证明 R≥3 时此类码至多有限多个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nearly-perfect covering codes 是覆盖码中对应于 nearly-perfect error-correcting codes 的重要对象。已有研究完成了覆盖半径 R=1 的分类，但对 R≥2 的存在性、界的紧性以及分类情况仍不清楚。本文旨在澄清原始 Van Wee 界的局限，并建立更精确的理论框架。

Method: 作者首先分析原始 Van Wee bound 的假设与缺陷，证明其未考虑码的最小距离，因此当 R≥2 时不存在达到该界等号的码。随后引入依赖最小距离的改进界，并构造满足改进界等号的 nearly-perfect covering codes。进一步通过证明这些码的结构性质，对小覆盖半径情形进行分类。

Result: 论文证明：1）当 R≥2 时，没有码能达到原始 Van Wee bound 的等号；2）给出了考虑最小距离的 refined bound；3）展示了一些达到改进界的 nearly-perfect covering codes；4）证明 R=2,3 的所有 nearly-perfect covering codes 都与所构造的码等价；5）证明任意 R≥3 时，此类码数量至多有限。

Conclusion: 本文修正了 nearly-perfect covering codes 研究中原始 Van Wee 界的不足，建立了更精确的界并完成了 R=2,3 的分类结果，同时给出高覆盖半径下的有限性结论，为覆盖码理论提供了更严格的结构性理解。

Abstract: We study (binary) nearly-perfect covering codes, which are codes that attain the Van Wee bound with equality. They act as the covering counterparts to nearly-perfect error-correcting codes, which attain the Johnson bound with equality. These codes have been completely classified for covering radius $R=1$. We prove that no code with $R\geq 2$ can attain the original Van Wee bound with equality, since it omits the dependence on the minimum distance of the code. We refine the bound to account for the minimum distance and show some nearly-perfect covering codes. By proving some structural properties of such codes, we prove all nearly-perfect covering codes with $R=2,3$ must be equivalent to the codes we showed. We also prove that for any $R\geq 3$, there are at most a finite number of nearly-perfect covering codes.

</details>


### [252] [A Theory of Probabilistic Power Provisioning for Data Centers with Distributed Energy Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12993)
*Can Emre Koksal,Richard A. Barry,Artun Sel*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出用于数据中心储能辅助供电配置的概率框架，刻画功率预留、储能容量与过载概率之间的关系，并验证存在小电池与大电池两个运行区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 工作负载功耗需求增长且波动显著，使数据中心供电容量成为关键约束；分布式储能可降低为随机负载预留的供电容量，但其收益取决于负载统计特征和时间尺度，因此需要统一的建模与配置方法。

Method: 作者建立概率分析框架，联合描述预留功率、储能容量和过载概率；区分 Small Battery Region 与 Large Battery Region；在大电池区间提出 effective power 概念，类似 effective bandwidth，用于捕捉需求的时间相关性并给出渐近紧的功率需求刻画；进一步分析时间相关、空间聚合、多时间尺度负载对储能需求和统计复用收益的影响，并用三个生产数据中心的功率轨迹进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，来自 HPC、GPU 训练和云服务的数据中心负载虽具有异质性、周期平稳性和多模态特征，但均呈现理论预测的两个运行区间；一个简单的四参数两状态模型能够较好捕捉影响储能—功率权衡的关键动态。

Conclusion: 该框架为下一代 AI 数据中心的储能辅助供电配置提供了概率理论基础和实用容量规划原则，可指导在给定过载风险下权衡供电预留与储能容量。

Abstract: The growing power demands and variability of AI workloads make electrical power delivery a critical constraint in data-center operation. Distributed energy storage can reduce the power capacity required to support stochastic loads, but its benefits depend fundamentally on the statistics and time scales of demand. This paper develops a probabilistic framework that jointly characterizes provisioned power, energy-storage capacity, and the probability of overdraw. We show that storage-assisted provisioning separates into two operating regimes. In the Small Battery Region, overdraw is dominated by short-lived demand excursions and storage provides nearly linear reductions in the required power margin. In the Large Battery Region, overdraw results from sustained demand fluctuations over longer spans of time, and the required margin exhibits diminishing returns with storage. For this regime we introduce effective power, an analogue of effective bandwidth that captures the temporal statistics of the demand and gives an asymptotically tight characterization of the required power. We further quantify how temporal correlation and spatial aggregation affect storage requirements and statistical multiplexing gains, and extend the analysis to loads with multiple demand time scales. Finally, we evaluate the framework using power-demand traces from three production data centers spanning HPC, GPU-training, and cloud-service workloads. Despite their heterogeneous, cyclo-stationary and multi-modal behavior, the measured workloads exhibit the predicted regimes, and a simple four-parameter two-state model captures the dynamics governing their storage-power tradeoffs. The resulting framework provides both a probabilistic foundation and practical dimensioning principles for storage-assisted power provisioning in next-generation AI data centers.

</details>


### [253] [Deterministic Johnson--Lindenstrauss Projections from Pisot $β$-Transformations for Zero-Knowledge Private Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13078)
*I. Dey,I. Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于 Pisot β-变换标准化轨道的公开确定性投影，用于在零知识证明中降低嵌入维度并避免电路内随机性与随机矩阵承诺成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零知识语义类别证明需要比较高维嵌入与类别中心，成本随维度 d 增长；传统 JL 随机投影虽可降维，但随机矩阵的承诺、采样证明和潜在泄漏在电路中代价高。

Method: 构造由 Pisot β-变换标准化轨道生成的公开确定性投影，并通过 β-map 的谱隙与相关性的几何衰减分析其距离保持性质；证明平方范数估计的偏差随采样间隔几何衰减，方差为 V0/m。

Result: 在一个集中性假设下，方差常数 V0 在理论上与维度无关；实验中也表现为维度无关。通过搜索可找到一个公开种子同时保持所有类别中心的两两距离。与六种标准投影相比，统计质量接近测量噪声水平。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持 JL 类降维效果的同时，避免了电路内随机性，支持固定有限域中的精确可复现实现，并具有较低的逐步生成成本，因此适合零知识证明场景。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge (ZK) proofs certify that a message belongs to an allowed semantic class without revealing the message, but the certificate compares a high-dimensional embedding against class centroids, so its cost grows with the embedding dimension $d$. A Johnson--Lindenstrauss (JL) projection lowers $d$ to $m\ll d$ while preserving pairwise distances, yet a random JL matrix must be committed and its sampling proved inside the circuit, which is costly and a leakage risk. We construct a public deterministic projection from the standardized orbit of a Pisot $β$-transformation, analyzed through the spectral gap of the $β$-map, the geometric decay of its correlations, rather than equidistribution. We prove that the induced squared-norm estimator is unbiased up to a term decaying geometrically with a sampling gap, and that its variance is $V_0/m$ with a constant $V_0$ that is dimension-free in experiment and, under one stated concentration hypothesis, in theory. A single public seed preserving all pairwise centroid distances therefore exists and is found by search. Against six standard projections, including the chaotic-sequence matrix of Yu \emph{et al.}, the construction matches statistical quality to within measurement noise, and it is the only one simultaneously free of in-circuit randomness and exactly reproducible in a fixed finite field at a per-step cost $\log_2β$ rather than $2^{k}$.

</details>


### [254] [Counterexamples to the Markovity Conjecture for the Two-Receiver Broadcast Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13170)
*Yanxiao Liu,Mian Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文摘要声称给出两个反例，推翻 Gohari、Liu 和 Nair 提出的 Markovity Conjecture，从而表明 Marton 内界相关对偶泛函的优化结构并不总满足猜想的马尔可夫形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Markovity Conjecture 若成立，将显著简化 Marton 内界的计算与刻画；因此验证其正确性或寻找反例对广播信道信息论具有结构性意义。

Method: 作者构造两个三元输入、转移概率严格为正的广播信道反例，均采用相同的非矩形 2×2 辅助随机变量结构，并显式给出非马尔可夫构造；随后与所有满足猜想马尔可夫结构的构造进行目标值比较。

Result: 摘要称所构造的非马尔可夫方案在目标函数值上被严格证明优于任何满足 Markovity Conjecture 结构的方案，因此形成两个反例。

Conclusion: 若证明无误，这些结果否定了 Markovity Conjecture，并说明 Marton 内界对偶优化器可能需要更一般的非马尔可夫辅助结构。

Abstract: We present two counterexamples to the \emph{Markovity Conjecture} of Gohari, Liu and Nair (ISIT 2025), which is a structural conjecture concerning the optimizers of the dual functional associated with Marton's inner bound and, if true, would greatly simplify the evaluation of Marton's inner bound. Both counterexamples are ternary-input broadcast channels with strictly positive transition probabilities and use the same nonrectangular $2\times2$ auxiliary structure. In each case, we exhibit an explicit non-Markov construction whose objective value is rigorously larger than that achievable by any construction satisfying the conjectured Markov structure. Both examples are obtained with the assistance of GPT-5.6 Sol and disprove the Markovity Conjecture.

</details>


### [255] [Age of Incorrect Information for Pull-Based State Estimation of General Markov Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13248)
*Marco Zanni,Mohamad Assaad,Touraj Soleymani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究多状态马尔可夫源的拉取式远程状态估计，在同时考虑信息新鲜度与正确性的 AoII 指标下，构建并求解单源与多源调度优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统远程估计常关注信息年龄或传输新鲜度，但仅新鲜并不保证估计正确；对于多状态马尔可夫源，需要一个同时刻画“过时程度”和“估计错误”的框架，并降低精确求解在状态空间与存储上的复杂度。

Method: 论文以折扣 AoII 为目标，在 MAP 估计下建立联合源状态-AoII 信念 MDP；证明可达信念可由最近一次成功观测的源状态和距该观测的时隙数表示；采用有限截断近似并给出误差界与截断选择准则。针对可靠链路给出等待时间查表型最优策略；针对不可靠链路提出持久化策略和性能界；进一步提出早期平稳切换的混合估计器以节省内存。多源场景被建模为 restless multi-armed bandit，并基于可指标化条件与插值构造近似 Whittle 指数策略。

Result: 数值实验展示了单源最优策略结构，验证了多源调度策略的性能，并表明所提出的启发式策略在显著降低计算与存储开销的同时，性能接近最优解。

Conclusion: 该工作为考虑正确性与新鲜度的拉取式远程估计提供了系统的 MDP/指数策略框架，并通过信念压缩、截断误差控制、策略结构化和近似指数方法提升了可计算性与可扩展性。

Abstract: We study pull-based remote state estimation of an arbitrary, multi-state Markov source while accounting for both freshness and correctness attributes of information. To that end, we formulate a discounted optimization problem in terms of the age of incorrect information (AoII), and express it as a joint source-AoII belief Markov decision process (MDP) under maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation. We then exploit the information structure of the model and prove that every reachable belief is represented by the last successfully observed source state and the number of time slots elapsed since that observation. For numerical computation, we truncate the elapsed no-success duration at a finite level and derive an explicit error bound and a criterion for selecting the truncation parameter. For reliable links, we show that an optimal policy can be represented by a look-up table of waiting times. For unreliable links, we propose a persistent policy and derive computable performance bounds. We also show that the MAP estimate stabilizes after a finite number of time slots. To further reduce memory requirements, we introduce a hybrid estimator with an early stationary switch and derive a computable bound on the resulting difference in performance. Finally, we extend the framework to multiple sources, formulate the scheduling problem as a restless multi-armed bandit, establish a sufficient condition for indexability, and develop an approximate Whittle index policy based on interpolation. Our numerical results illustrate the structure of the optimal single-source policy, evaluate the performance of the multi-source policies, and verify that the proposed heuristic policies closely approach the optimal solution while substantially reducing computational efforts.

</details>


### [256] [Impedance-Aware Zonal Port Activation for Fluid Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13249)
*Yuanhui Wu,Hao Jiang,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向有限端口流体天线阵列的激活方法，在速率、孔径质量与可行性约束之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线阵列端口激活会同时影响多用户预编码的有效信道和物理孔径稀疏结构。单纯基于信道的选择可能集中高增益端口而损害孔径质量；单纯基于几何的选择又不能利用瞬时CSI。因此需要一种兼顾信道增益、阵列孔径特性、RF链预算与电磁可行性的端口激活策略。

Method: 作者将有限端口FAA激活建模为速率—孔径—可行性优化问题，并要求满足精确RF链数量约束。提出IA-ZPA方法：使用紧凑的CSI条件端口评分器对端口排序；通过棋盘式可行性投影保证激活结构满足孔径约束；在推理阶段引入互阻抗感知选择；最后采用独立的电流域RZF后端评估源驱动可行性。

Result: 在统一的感应电动势协议下，IA-ZPA在满足给定平均PSLL目标的方法中取得最高的受约束速率，同时其决策时间显著低于贪婪选择方法。

Conclusion: IA-ZPA表明，将学习型信道感知评分与确定性几何/可行性投影及互阻抗建模相结合，可以有效提升FAA端口激活的通信性能、孔径质量和推理效率。

Abstract: Fluid antenna array (FAA) activation jointly determines the effective multi-user channel for precoding and the sparse physical aperture. Channel-oriented selection can concentrate high-gain ports and erode aperture quality, whereas geometry-oriented selection does not adapt to instantaneous channel state information (CSI). This paper formulates finite-port FAA activation as a rate--aperture--feasibility problem under an exact RF-chain budget. We propose impedance-aware zonal port activation (IA-ZPA), which couples compact CSI-conditioned port scoring with a checkerboard feasibility projection and inference-time mutual-impedance-aware selection. The learned scorer ranks ports, while the deterministic rule fixes the active aperture; a separate current-domain RZF backend then evaluates source-drive feasibility. Under a common induced-EMF protocol, IA-ZPA attains the largest constrained rate among the methods satisfying the prescribed mean-PSLL target with a substantially lower decision time than greedy selection.

</details>


### [257] [Resource-efficient Semantic Coding Schemes with Manifold-constrained Hyper-connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13253)
*Jingwen Fu,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种带熵瓶颈的流形约束超连接编码方案，用于在无线信道中实现资源高效且鲁棒的语义通信与任务导向通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信和任务导向通信需要在压缩传输信息的同时抵抗信道噪声与衰落；现有学习式收发机通常通过增大编码器或提高信道特征维度提升可靠性，但会增加计算复杂度和信道占用，因此需要显式码率控制来平衡性能、带宽和功率资源。

Method: 提出 manifold-constrained hyper-connection（mHC）语义编码器，将传统单残差路径扩展为多残差流，并用双随机混合矩阵约束流间交互；同时引入熵瓶颈对信道特征量化并估计熵编码码率，从而进行端到端率失真或任务性能优化。论文还从理论上说明双随机约束的流混合不会增加传输特征的微分熵。

Result: 在 AWGN、Rayleigh 衰落、Rician 衰落以及不完美 CSI 条件下，实验显示该方案相比残差结构和无约束超连接基线，在语义/任务性能、通信鲁棒性和收敛稳定性方面均有提升，且不增加额外信道使用。

Conclusion: mHC 与熵瓶颈结合能够在不显著增加参数量、浮点开销和信道占用的情况下，提高语义通信和任务导向通信系统的资源效率、鲁棒性与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Semantic communication (SemCom) and task-oriented communication (TOC) can reduce wireless resource consumption by focusing on transmitting semantic or task-relevant information instead of raw messages. In practice, a main challenge is to make transmitting information robust to channel noise and fading while keeping it compact. Existing learning-based transceivers often improve reliability by using larger encoders or higher-dimensional channel features, which increase computation complexity and channel uses. Therefore, optimized system design needs explicit rate control to balance performance and transmitting resources e.g., bandwidth and power. For this purpose, we propose a manifold-constrained hyper-connection (mHC) coding scheme with an entropy bottleneck (EB) for resource-efficient SemCom and TOC over wireless channels. Instead of using a single residual path of existing encoders, the proposed mHC-based semantic encoder applies multiple residual streams and constrains their interaction by doubly stochastic (DS) mixing matrices. The new structure improves representation diversity and training stability with negligible parameter and floating-point overhead. The EB quantizes the channel features and estimates the entropy-coded rate, enabling end-to-end rate--distortion/task optimization under bandwidth and transmit-power constraints. We further show that DS-constrained stream mixing does not increase the differential entropy of the transmitted features. This implies no increase in the ideal EB coding length. Experiments on SemCom and TOC under additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN), Rayleigh fading, Rician fading, and imperfect channel state information (CSI) show that the proposed scheme improves semantic/task performance, communication robustness, and convergence stability over residual and unconstrained HC baselines, while requiring no additional channel uses.

</details>


### [258] [EM-Guided Graph Learning for Fluid Antenna Beamforming under Current-Domain Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13254)
*Yuanhui Wu,Hao Jiang,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究紧凑孔径下流体天线阵列的端口布局与电流域下行多用户波束成形联合优化，提出电磁引导图网络，在通信性能与电磁可实现性之间实现可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧凑孔径中，仅由信道驱动的端口放置容易导致端口聚集，从而增强互耦、恶化辐射条件数、提高源电压需求，并造成电流负载不均衡。因此需要一种同时考虑通信增益和电磁约束的端口配置与传输设计方法。

Method: 提出一种电磁引导的图神经网络，根据信道观测预测端口布局，并利用几何信息与互阻抗信息进一步细化布局；同时联合优化端口位置与电流域传输/波束成形。训练目标同时纳入通信性能指标和电磁可行性指标，并采用统一评估流程与其他方法比较。

Result: 在统一的可行性标准下，所提方法能够在通信速率、电流负载和配置时延之间提供可调节的折中表现。

Conclusion: 将电磁先验引入图网络并与通信目标联合优化，有助于缓解紧凑流体天线阵列中的互耦与负载不均问题，使系统设计更符合实际电磁可实现性。

Abstract: Fluid antenna arrays (FAAs) reconfigure a finite set of radiating ports within a prescribed aperture. In compact apertures, however, channel-driven placement may cluster ports, strengthen mutual coupling, degrade radiation conditioning, increase source-voltage demand, and produce uneven current loading. This paper studies downlink multi-user beamforming with jointly optimized port placement and current-domain transmission. An electromagnetic-guided graph network predicts port layouts from channel observations and refines them using geometric and mutual-impedance information. The training objective jointly considers communication performance and electromagnetic feasibility, while a common evaluation procedure is applied to all methods. The results show that, under a common feasibility standard, the proposed method provides a controllable tradeoff among communication rate, current loading, and configuration latency.

</details>


### [259] [MiLAC-Aided Beamforming for MIMO Over-the-Air Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13353)
*Yaru Wang,Deyou Zhang,Qingchao Li,Jun Liu,Chuang Shi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究利用微波线性模拟计算机（MiLAC）辅助的波束成形来降低 MIMO AirComp 的射频链硬件开销，并在保持接近全数字波束成形精度的同时提升聚合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AirComp 可实现低时延无线数据聚合，但在衰落信道和接收噪声下，信号对齐不完美会限制聚合精度。全数字 MIMO 波束成形虽能改善精度，却需要每根天线配置一条 RF 链，硬件成本高。因此需要一种低 RF 链数量、同时保持高聚合精度的方案。

Method: 在无损且互易的 MiLAC 模型下，论文联合优化发射端数字预编码矩阵与接收端 MiLAC 聚合矩阵，以最小化均方误差（MSE）。作者提出交替优化算法：发射预编码矩阵通过 KKT 条件和二分搜索进行最优更新；接收端聚合矩阵对应的凸子问题则通过投影梯度下降进行全局求解。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提算法的收敛性。结果表明，MiLAC 辅助波束成形在显著减少 RF 链数量的情况下，可接近全数字波束成形的 MSE 性能，并且在相同 RF 链预算下优于基于移相器的混合波束成形。

Conclusion: MiLAC 辅助波束成形为 MIMO AirComp 提供了一种兼顾硬件效率与聚合精度的有效架构，有望在低时延、低复杂度无线数据聚合场景中替代传统高成本全数字方案。

Abstract: Over-the-air computation (AirComp) enables low-latency wireless data aggregation, but its accuracy is limited by imperfect signal alignment over fading channels and receiver noise. Fully digital beamforming improves aggregation accuracy in multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) AirComp systems but requires one radio-frequency (RF) chain per antenna. To reduce this hardware burden, we investigate microwave linear analog computer (MiLAC)-aided beamforming for MIMO AirComp. Under a lossless and reciprocal MiLAC model, we jointly optimize the transmit digital precoding matrices and the receive-side MiLAC aggregation matrix to minimize the mean squared error (MSE). An alternating optimization algorithm is developed, in which the precoding matrices are optimally updated using the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker conditions and bisection, while the resulting convex aggregation matrix subproblem is solved globally using projected gradient descent. Numerical results verify the algorithm's convergence and demonstrate that MiLAC-aided beamforming approaches the MSE performance of fully digital beamforming with substantially fewer RF chains and outperforms phase-shifter-based hybrid beamforming under the same RF-chain budget.

</details>
